<script type="text/javascript">// control touch "shop" in nav 
if ($(window).width() > 580 && $(window).width() < 991){// between 580 and 991 for touch case
   var content = document.querySelector(".nav .parent .has-category");  
    var content_sub =  document.querySelector(".nav .sub-apmegamenu-1");  
    content.addEventListener("touchstart", function(e){  
      e.preventDefault();
      if(content_sub.style.visibility=="hidden"){    
        e.preventDefault();
       content_sub.style.visibility="visible"  ; 
      }
     else{
       e.preventDefault();
       content_sub.style.visibility="hidden"  ; 
      }
})   
 }
</script>

<h5 class="name">
<a class="product-name" href="/products/uni-t-ut123-auto-ranging-digital-multimeter" title="UNI-T UT123 Auto-Ranging Digital Multimeter">UNI-T UT123 Auto-Ranging Digital Multimeter</a>
</h5>
<div id="uni-t-ut123-auto-ranging-digital-multimeter" style="display:none;"><p><strong>Auto-Ranging Readout</strong></p><p>Auto-ranging is a powerful advantage to have on your multimeter because it saves you the hassle of having to know which range of value the resistance, voltage or whatever electrical characteristic you're finding falls under. The UT123 multimeter can find the value for you.</p><p><strong>EBTN Display</strong></p><p>Enhanced Black Twisted Nematic (EBTN) LCDs has a very dark background with low light leakage, which allows for a very wide viewing angle, higher contrast, and better definition. With a very wide operating temperature, the display does not require any temperature compensation when the temperature goes down at 14 degrees Fahrenheit.</p><p><strong>Safe NCV Testing</strong></p><p>A non-contact voltage tester is the safest way to make sure the power is off without touching any wires. This is where the UT123 tester comes in handy. The tester will light up and make noise when it comes close to a hot (live) wire, and the overload protection function provides you the highest security during working. (CAT III 600V)</p><p><strong>Versatile Functionality</strong></p><p>With features including DC/AC voltage, resistance, continuity, and temperature readouts in degrees Celsius or Fahrenheit, the UT123 is ideal for electrical settings as well as automotive and household electrical problem testing.</p><p><strong>Portable &amp; Smart</strong></p><p>With a small form factor, this multimeter conveniently fits inside your pocket so it’s always ready when you need it. You can press the 'hold' button to maintain the real-time readings when you are unable to record the readouts. Also, the multimeter will automatically alert you when the battery is low, which can be a huge hassle when you are out working in the field.</p><p> </p></div>

<h5 class="name">
<a class="product-name" href="/products/creality3d-cr-10-max-3d-printer" title="Creality3D CR-10 Max 3D Printer">Creality3D CR-10 Max 3D Printer</a>
</h5>
<div id="creality3d-cr-10-max-3d-printer" style="display:none;"><p><strong>Large Build Volume</strong></p>
<p>Often it is believed that personal FDM 3D printers are limited to only making relatively smaller models, but CR-10 MAX has a generous print volume of 450 x 450 x 470 mm, which allows printing larger models and turning your ideas into reality.</p>
<p><strong>Stability Triangle Frame</strong></p>
<p>The Z-axis brace is linking to the top of the frame to the base, leading to the stability triangle frame. This unique design improves the printing precision due to reducing the error produced by the Z-wobble.</p>
<p><strong>Matrix Automatic Leveling</strong></p>
<p>CR-10 MAX comes with an automatic bed leveling feature, ensures that the printing platform is level whenever you’re preparing to print. With an inductive proximity sensor near the tip of the print head that “probes” specific 16 points on the platform when the bed leveling process is first initiated.</p>
<p><strong>Double Belts &amp; Nozzles</strong></p>
<p>CR-10 MAX equips two belts with a powerful biaxial motor, making the printing process stable. Compatible with two different nozzles, the 0.4mm for high precision prints while plus a 0.8mm nozzle for faster prints.</p>
<p><strong>Split Flow Power for Heated Bed</strong></p>
<p>One intriguing innovation feature for this printer is a split flow 24V power supply for its large heated bed, which reduces electromagnetic interference and improves the heating efficiency.</p></div>

</h5>
<div id="sainsmart-uv-curing-chamber-for-sla-dlp-resin-3d-printer" style="display:none;"><p><strong><span class="a-list-item">Easy To Use</span></strong></p>
<p><span class="a-list-item">The SainSmart UV curing chamber adopts a special control system, which makes the operating process easy and effective. The three-side light source and heating function can be turned on and off separately, realizing the combination of four modes to meet your different needs.</span></p>
<p><strong><span class="a-list-item">Automatic Temperature Control</span></strong></p>
<p><span class="a-list-item">Accurate temperature control is the key to successful post-curing. Both the chamber inner and the lamp beads can achieve smart temperature control, which ensures the stable performance of functional resin-printed models and extends the service life of the lamp beads. </span></p>
<p><strong><span class="a-list-item">Fast Heating</span></strong></p>
<p><span class="a-list-item">The heating chamber can be heated to 60 °C, speeding up the curing process and to maximize some of the physical properties of the resin-printed models. </span></p>
<p><strong><span class="a-list-item">Uniform UV Curing</span></strong></p>
<p><span class="a-list-item">In order to produce the uniform light intensity of 405nm wavelength from the 40 LEDs light source, the UV-LED array stage has been kept rotating during exposure, which enables all angles of your print to be cured and exposed perfectly. </span></p>
<p><strong><span class="a-list-item">Ideal for 3D Resin Prints</span></strong></p>
<p><span class="a-list-item">By delivering high-quality results and compatible with most of the SLA and DLP printers on the market, SainSmart gives you an ideal choice for your 3D resin prints. Also, this UV curing chamber is a cost-effective option due to its compact design and excellent UV-LED 3D curing capability.</span></p></div>

<div class="product-meta">
<div class="clearfix">
<h5 class="name">
<a class="product-name" href="/products/toolpac-ts80-smart-soldering-iron-usb-type-c-power" title="ToolPAC TS80 Smart Soldering Iron, USB Type-C Power">ToolPAC TS80 Smart Soldering Iron, USB Type-C Power</a>
</h5>
<div id="toolpac-ts80-smart-soldering-iron-usb-type-c-power" style="display:none;"><p>TS80 is a newly designed smart soldering iron, suitable for hobbyists, tinkerers, or electrical engineers doing the most precise repair jobs.</p>
<p><strong>Smart</strong></p>
<p>The integrated STM32 processor automatically regulates the TS80’s “sleep”, “standby”, and “operating” modes, ensuring a safe operation for the user. Equipped with an OLED screen, giving feedback on the TS80’s operation status.</p>
<p><strong>More Portable</strong></p>
<p>Driven by the pursuit of perfection, TS80 is a smaller and thinner soldering iron than its ancestor <strong><a href="https://www.sainsmart.com/collections/tools-instruments/products/2018-upgraded-toolpac-pro32-smart-soldering-tool-set" rel="noopener" target="_blank">TS100</a></strong>. This unit is powered by USB Type-C interface and QC3.0 standard input. It can be powered using any mobile power supply that meets the QC3.0 (9V 2A) protocol. TS80 is ready for work anytime! </p>
<p><strong>More Durable</strong></p>
<p>The body of the TS80 is made of a CNC machined aluminum alloy. This provides you with a small, but robust package designed to last!</p>
<p><strong>Excellent Temperature Control</strong></p>
<p>The internal ceramic heating core and pure copper heating conductor focuses the heat to the iron’s tip, ensuring easy, reliable, and consistent operation for all jobs!</p>
<p><strong>Code Application Layer Open Source</strong></p>
<p>The TS80’s firmware is open source, allowing for customized adjustment of the iron’s parameters and behavior!</p>

<div id="sainsmart-x-creality3d-ender-3-3d-printer" style="display:none;"><p><strong>Cost Effective</strong></p>
<p>By eliminating the high retail markup, SainSmart is able to offer a sensible package with unrivaled quality in the entry-level space. The compact design allows Ender-3 to fit in the trunk of your car or even back seat,  while still providing 6x more build volume than other entry-level printers.</p>
<p><strong>High Precision &amp; Noiseless</strong></p>
<p>The CNC-machined Y-axis mounting slot ensures highly precise positioning of the printer head. V-slot POM (polyoxymethylene) wheels allow the nozzle to glide smoothly and silently.</p>
<p><strong>No Clogging or Warping</strong></p>
<p>Our patented extruder design greatly reduces the risk of a clogged nozzle. There’s no need for printing tape or glue due to the incorporation of a new viscous platform sticker.</p>
<p><strong>Fast Heating &amp; Resume Print Feature</strong></p>
<p>The heated bed can reach its operating temperature in just five minutes. The printer is shielded by its power supply from voltage spikes and power outages. If electrical power is lost, prints can be resumed from the last layer, saving time and reducing waste.</p>
<p><strong>Semi-Assembled</strong></p>
<p>This easy-to-setup kit comes partially assembled, allowing you to learn about the basic construction of 3D printers as you finish putting it together. A fun STEM educational experience in mechanical engineering and electronics.</p>

<div id="6-axis-desktop-robotic-arm-assembled" style="display:none;"><p><strong>Production Quality</strong></p>
<p>Structure consists of different thickness of CNC machined PVC plates, making it a  durable and quality product guaranteed. The main joints utilize metal gear servos (MG966R and SG90), ensuring a reliable and smooth operation.</p>
<p><strong>Motion Flexibility</strong></p>
<p>Quality servos, precision structure, and 6 axis design allow an immense range of motion and flexibility within its reasonable limits.</p>
<p><strong>Programmable</strong></p>
<p>Servos are completely programmable using any microcontroller and code. We provide a lot of source code on our wiki webpage(<strong><a href="http://wiki.sainsmart.com/index.php/DIY_6-Axis_Servos_Control_Palletizing_Robot_Arm_Model_for_Arduino_UNO_MEGA2560#Introduction" rel="noopener" target="_blank">SainSmart Wiki</a></strong>)for the Uno R3, making this unit suitable for beginners! (Uno R3 and Shield are not included; purchase here <span style="color: #3366ff;"><a href="https://www.sainsmart.com/products/uno-r3-atmega328p-pu-16u2-arduino-compatible" rel="noopener" style="color: #3366ff;" target="_blank"><strong>UnoR3</strong></a></span> and <span style="color: #3366ff;"><a href="https://www.sainsmart.com/collections/shields/products/sensor-proto-shield-for-arduino" rel="noopener" style="color: #3366ff;" target="_blank"><strong>Shield</strong></a></span>)</p>
<p>All the parts are fully assembled, allowing for immediate programming and use.</p>
<p><strong>Stem Education</strong></p>
<p>Simulate this real palletizing robot arm and learn about the basic construction of a real robot arm. A fun STEM educational experience in mechanical engineering and electronics.</p></div>

</h5>
<div id="toolpac-pro32-smart-soldering-tool-set-1" style="display:none;"><p>The Pro32 is a staple tool for the hobbyist, tinkerer, or electrical engineer.  Featuring a sleek body, complete with temperature adjustment buttons and an OLED display, the Pro32 feels good in the hand and offers a wide temperature range for operation.  Its internal heating element warms up to operating temperature in just 12 seconds!</p>
<p><strong>Rapid Warmup</strong></p>
<p>With the help of dual temperature sensors, the internal heating element reaches its operating temperature extremely fast—just 12 seconds!  The tip temperature can be set between 212° F and 752° F (100° C to 400° C) using the adjustment buttons and OLED display.</p>
<p><strong>New Power Supply Design</strong></p>
<p>The Pro32 features an integrated DC5525 12-24V connection.  Its anti-static design isolates the temperature sensors from extraneous surges.</p>
<p><strong>Tough &amp; Portable</strong></p>
<p>The sleek, slim design is comprised of an SAE 304 stainless steel core, housed inside a polycarbonate shell with an easy-to-read OLED display.</p>
<p><strong>Smart &amp; Safe</strong></p>
<p>The integrated STM32 processor chip automatically regulates the Pro32’s “sleep mode”, “standby”, and “operating mode”, ensuring the safety of the user.</p></div>

<script type="text/javascript">//home load more content
$(function(){
  loadmorefun();
   loadmorefunbestseller();
  function loadmorefun(){
  if ( $(window).width() < 769){
      $("#loadmorenewlist").click(function(){ 
      $(this).hide();
      $("#productlist01 .item").addClass("show");
      });
  }
  else{
     $("#productlist01 .item").removeClass("show");
  }
  }
  
    function loadmorefunbestseller(){
  if ( $(window).width() < 769){
      $("#loadmorebestseller").click(function(){ 
      $(this).hide();
      $("#productrecommendlist .item").addClass("show");
      });
  }
  else{
     $("#productrecommendlist .item").removeClass("show");
  }
  }
  
  $(window).resize(function() { 
   loadmorefun();
    loadmorefunbestseller();
   });  
});              
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">  
    Shopify.doNotTriggerClickOnThumb = true;
    function changeImageQuickView(img, selector) {
        var src = $(img).attr("src");
        src = src.replace("_compact", "");
        $(selector).attr("src", src);
    }
    var selectCallbackQuickView = function(variant, selector) {
        var productItem = jQuery('.quick-view-product .product-item');
            addToCart = productItem.find('.add_to_cart_detail'),
            productPrice = productItem.find('.price'),
            comparePrice = productItem.find('.old-price'),
            totalPrice = productItem.find('.total-price span');
        if (variant) {
            if (variant.available) {
				addToCart.removeClass('disabled').removeAttr('disabled');
          		$(addToCart).find("span").text("Add to Cart");
            } else {
                addToCart.addClass('disabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
          		$(addToCart).find("span").text("Sold Out");
            }       
            productPrice.html(Shopify.formatMoney(variant.price, "£{{amount}}"));
            if ( variant.compare_at_price > variant.price ) {
                comparePrice
                    .html(Shopify.formatMoney(variant.compare_at_price, "£{{amount}}")).show();         
                productPrice.addClass('on-sale');
            } else {
                comparePrice.hide();
                productPrice.removeClass('on-sale');
            }
 
			   // BEGIN SWATCHES
            var form = jQuery('#' + selector.domIdPrefix).closest('form');
            for (var i=0,length=variant.options.length; i<length; i++) {
                var radioButton = form.find('.swatch[data-option-index="' + i + '"] :radio[value="' + variant.options[i] +'"]');
                if (radioButton.size()) {
                    radioButton.get(0).checked = true;
                }
            }
            // END SWATCHES
            updatePricingQuickView();
            
            /*begin variant image*/
            if (variant && variant.featured_image) {
                var originalImage = $("#product-featured-image-quickview");
                var newImage = variant.featured_image;
                var element = originalImage[0];
                Shopify.Image.switchImage(newImage, element, function (newImageSizedSrc, newImage, element) {
                    $('#thumblist_quickview img').each(function() {
                        var parentThumbImg = $(this).parent();
                        var productImage = $(this).parent().data("image");
                        if (newImageSizedSrc.includes(productImage)) {
                            $(this).parent().trigger('click');
                            return false;
                        }
                    });
                });
            }
        } else {
            addToCart.addClass('disabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
            $(addToCart).find("span").text("Unavailable");
        }
    };
                                           
</script>

            }, 300);
  
});

<script type="text/javascript">
window.addEventListener('load', function(){

    function getPageType() {
    
        return "Homepage";
    
}


    window.dataLayer.push({
        visitorType: "Guest"
    });



    window.dataLayer.push({
        event: "HomeView",
        pageType: "home"
    });



    

    

    

    

    Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/products/"]'))
        .forEach(function(select) {
            select.addEventListener('click', handleProductClick);
        });

    function getClickedProductHandle(element) {
        var arr = element.href.split('/products/');
        return arr[arr.length-1];
    }

    function handleProductClick(event) {
        if(typeof allProducts == "undefined") return;
        var target = event.target.matches('a[href*="/products/"]')
            ? event.target
            : event.target.closest('a[href*="/products/"]');
        var handle = getClickedProductHandle(target);
        var clickedProduct = allProducts.filter(function(product) {
            return product.handle === handle;
        });
        if (clickedProduct[0]) delete clickedProduct[0].list;
        if (clickedProduct[0]) delete clickedProduct[0].handle;
        window.dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'productClick',
            'ecommerce': {
                currencyCode: "USD",
                'click': {
                    'actionField': {'list': getPageType()},
                    'products': clickedProduct

                }
            },
        });
    }
}, false);
</script>
